Stack & surfaces\n\nTwo independently deployed TypeScript services. **`nextjs/`** is a Next.js 15.5 App Router app on Vercel (React 18, next-intl v4 with 40 locales, HeroUI/Tailwind, Drizzle ORM over `node-postgres`, Better Auth with a magic-link plugin plus a custom `license-key` sign-in plugin, tRPC v10 with superjson, Stripe v20 + a legacy Polar SDK path, Resend for email, Upstash Redis for rate limiting). It is the **system of record for money**: license keys (\"Account Keys\"), the `credit_grants` ledger, the `credit_balances` denormalized cache, and `stripe_processed_events` idempotency table all live here, exposed to the outside world through 23 REST route handlers plus one tRPC catch-all. **`hyperwhisper-cloud/`** is a Bun/Hono service on Fly.io that proxies audio to 11 STT providers and 7 LLM providers; it holds **no database** — it authenticates by POSTing the license key to `nextjs`'s `/api/license/validate`, caches the `{isValid, credits}` verdict in Upstash Redis for 1h (`license:<key>`), and charges usage by POSTing back to `/api/license/credits`. Trust boundaries: the Fly service trusts only the TCP peer address (`fdaa::/16` ⇒ off-edge ⇒ IP is `unknown`); the Next.js service trusts the Stripe signature, an `x-internal-secret` header, a Better Auth session cookie, and — critically — **the bare license key as a bearer credential** for all `/api/license/*` and `/api/account/*` endpoints. Shared mutable state: `inFlightDeductions: Set<Promise>` (cloud, drained on SIGTERM), the `_jwtClient`/`_inflight` Google token singletons, the ~1h in-memory model-list cache in `nextjs/lib/services/model-list.ts`, and the Redis license cache which both services write. Concurrency points: fire-and-forget `deductCredits()` after response flush, `costPromise.then(...)` after an SSE stream, the Deepgram WebSocket bridge, Stripe webhook re-deliveries, and `db.transaction` blocks in `db-layer.ts`.\n\n## Entry points\n\n**hyperwhisper-cloud (Hono, `src/index.ts`)**\n- `GET /health`, `GET /warmup` — unauthenticated, no input.\n- `POST /transcribe` (`src/routes/transcribe.ts`) — raw `audio/*` body; `X-STT-Provider`/`X-STT-Model`/`X-STT-Domain` headers; `account_key`|`license_key`, `language`, `initial_prompt`, `mode` query params read via `rawQuery()`. Buffers up to 2 GB into RAM.\n- `POST /post-process` (`src/routes/post-process.ts`) — JSON `{text ≤100k chars, prompt, account_key|license_key}`; `X-LLM-Provider`/model headers.\n- `POST /assistant` (`src/routes/assistant.ts`) — `multipart/form-data`: `account_key`, `messages` (JSON, ≤6 MB, may contain inline base64 images), `prompt`, `image` file (≤10 MB). Returns an SSE stream from Anthropic.\n- `GET /usage` (`src/routes/usage.ts`) — `account_key`|`license_key`|`identifier`, `force_refresh` query params.\n- `GET /ws/streaming-deepgram` (`src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts`) — WebSocket upgrade; `account_key`, `language`, `vocabulary` query params; binary audio frames proxied to `wss://api.deepgram.com`.\n- `SIGTERM`/`SIGINT` handler — drains `inFlightDeductions` for 4 s.\n- `hyperwhisper-cloud/scripts/smoke-test.ts` — operator-run, hits the live service.\n\n**nextjs — public REST (all unauthenticated unless noted)**\n- `POST /api/license/validate` + alias `/api/account/validate` — `{license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name}`. IP rate-limited 30/min. Falls back to a live Polar API call and can **mint a license + 5000 credits** on a DB miss.\n- `POST /api/license/activate` + `/api/account/activate` — same shape, rate-limited, legacy.\n- `POST /api/license/deactivate` + `/api/account/deactivate` — stub, always 200.\n- `GET /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `?license_key=`; returns balance. **No rate limit.**\n- `POST /api/license/credits` + `/api/account/credits` — `{license_key, amount, metadata}`; **atomically deducts `amount` credits. No rate limit, no auth beyond the key.** This is the sole write path used by the Fly service.\n- `POST /api/checkout/credits` — `{amount: 5..500, licenseKey?, email?}`; creates Stripe customers and Checkout Sessions. **No auth, no rate limit.**\n- `GET /api/checkout` — redirect to `/credits`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/stripe` — Stripe signature verified; handles `checkout.session.completed`, `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded`, `checkout.session.async_payment_failed`, `charge.refunded`.\n- `POST /api/webhooks/add-blog-post` — Outrank bearer token (+ optional HMAC); upserts `blog_posts` and calls `revalidatePath`.\n- `GET /api/download` — `?platform=&arch=`; fetches an appcast XML and builds a dynamic `RegExp` from `arch`.\n- `GET /api/config`, `GET /api/internal/models`, `GET /models` — cached public JSON; `/models` and `/api/internal/models` fan out to 6 paid provider list APIs on a cache miss.\n- `GET|POST /api/auth/[...all]` — Better Auth (magic link, `/sign-in/license-key` at 5/min).\n- `GET|POST /api/trpc/[trpc]` — `download.*` (public), `customer.*` (protected), `admin.*` (admin).\n- `GET /api/customer/profile` — Better Auth session required.\n- `GET /[locale]/user/auth/sign-out` — route handler.\n\n**nextjs — internal REST (`x-internal-secret` header, timing-safe)**\n- `POST /api/internal/grant-license` — mints a key + credits for an email.\n- `POST /api/internal/granted-emails` — bulk email dump.\n- `POST /api/internal/licenses-for-email` — keys + balances for an email.\n\n**nextjs — middleware & rendering**\n- `middleware.ts` — matcher `/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\..*).*)`. Gates `/{locale}/user/customers` with a real `fetch('/api/auth/get-session')` + `role === \"admin\"`; gates all other `/{locale}/user/*` on **cookie presence only**; runs `next-intl` for everything else.\n- 22 `page.tsx` files under `app/[locale]/`, plus `sitemap.ts` / `robots.ts`.\n\n## Invariants (ranked)\n\n### I1: Every credit reservation taken at preflight is ≥ the total cost actually deducted for that request, across all retries, fallbacks and re-prompts.\nWhy it matters: the reservation is the only thing standing between a low-balance user and unbounded provider spend the business eats; under-reserving turns each request into a free-money bug at HyperWhisper's expense.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"ESTIMATED_POST_PROCESS_CREDITS\\|ESTIMATED_ASSISTANT_CREDITS\\|estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks\\|minimumStreamingCredits\"` — then trace each. `post-process.ts` reserves a **flat `1.0`** yet can spend `retriesFor(provider)` primary attempts (up to 3 for groq) + a fallback provider's full retry budget + a *prompt-leakage retry on a third provider* whose cost is `costUsd += retryResponse.costUsd`. `assistant.ts` reserves `3.0 × countInlineImages()` but the multipart `image` file adds an image `countInlineImages` never counts, and output tokens are unbounded. In `transcribe.ts`, cross-check `FALLBACK_CHAINS` against the `rates` array built in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks` — grep `costUsd +=`, `retriesFor(`, `callWithRetry(`.\n\n### I2: Every upstream call that has already cost money results in exactly one `deductCredits()`, on every exit path — including thrown-exception and stream-error paths — and never more than one.\nWhy it matters: a missed deduction is silent revenue loss at scale; a duplicated one double-charges a paying customer.\nSearch strategy: in `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/`, grep `deductCredits(` (7 call sites) and `return c.json\\|return errorResponse\\|return new Response`; for each `return` that follows a successful provider call, confirm a deduction precedes it. Specific suspects: `assistant.ts:350` — `costPromise.then(...)` bills only on resolve, so a rejected `costPromise` (Anthropic stream aborted mid-flight after tokens were billed) drops the charge entirely; `ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` `endSession()` is guarded by `sessionEnded` but is reachable from `onClose`, `onError`, and the Deepgram `close` listener concurrently. Also grep `performDeduction` — any call site bypassing `deductCredits()` escapes `inFlightDeductions` and is lost on SIGTERM.\n\n### I3: A `license:<key>` Redis entry with `isValid:false` is written only for a *definitive* verdict (HTTP 4xx from the licensing API); transient failures (5xx, timeout, network, unparseable body) must leave the cache untouched.\nWhy it matters: `LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS = 3600` — one Vercel cold start or a malformed body during a deploy locks a paying customer out of transcription for a full hour across every Fly region.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"cacheLicense(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src`. `middleware/auth.ts:70-79` implements the rule correctly (explicit `if (response.status >= 500) return ... ; // not caching`). `routes/usage.ts:44-52` (`validateLicenseAndGetCredits`) does **not**: it computes `isValid = data.valid === true` after `response.json().catch(() => ({}))` and unconditionally calls `cacheLicense` — so a 500, an HTML error page, or an empty body all poison the cache with `isValid:false`. Verify every `cacheLicense` call is preceded by a status check.\n\n### I4: Every STT adapter that returns a non-empty transcript returns a strictly positive `durationSeconds` (falling back to `estimateSecondsFromBytes` when the upstream duration field is absent or zero).\nWhy it matters: `costUsd` is `durationSeconds × rate` for duration-billed providers, so a missing field means a real, provider-billed transcription is charged **0 credits** — a free-transcription oracle triggered by any upstream response-shape change.\nSearch strategy: `grep -ln \"estimateSecondsFromBytes\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and diff against the 11 STT adapters. Present in `assemblyai.ts`, `mistral.ts`, `soniox.ts`, `openai.ts`, and `google-chirp.ts` (via its own `estimateAudioSeconds`); **absent in `deepgram.ts`, `groq.ts`, `elevenlabs.ts`, `xai-stt.ts`, `azure-mai.ts`**. For each, read where `durationSeconds` is derived (`data.metadata.duration`, `data.duration`, last word `.end`, `data.durationMilliseconds`) and confirm a guard before `compute*TranscriptionCost(...)`.\n\n### I5: Every Stripe webhook handler that mutates licenses or credits dedupes on a stable Stripe id before mutating, and returns non-2xx (so Stripe retries) on any *transient* failure.\nWhy it matters: Stripe delivers at-least-once and fires both `checkout.session.completed` and `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` for the same purchase; a missing guard double-grants credits, and swallowing an error returns 200 and permanently loses the grant.\nSearch strategy: in `nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts`, grep `grantCreditLot(\\|grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\\|refundCreditGrant(\\|updateAccountKey(`. `handleCreditPurchase` correctly routes through `grantCreditsForStripeEvent` (keyed on `stripe_processed_events.stripe_object_id`); `handleLicensePurchase:126` calls the **un-deduped `grantCreditLot({sourceType:\"license_bundle\", sourceId: session.id})`** and its only guard is a prior `findAccountByStripeSession` read — check whether `grantCreditLotInTransaction`'s `onConflictDoNothing` target actually covers `(sourceType, sourceId)`. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:121-130`: `handleChargeRefunded` errors are caught and the route still returns `{received:true}` — a refund clawback lost to a transient DB blip is never retried.\n\n### I6: No credit amount, price, or quantity that determines what a user is charged or granted is taken from client-controlled input without server-side re-derivation or a hard bound.\nWhy it matters: this is the direct path to minting free credits or draining another key's wallet.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"body.amount\\|input.amount\\|metadata.credit_amount\\|creditAmount\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib nextjs/server`. `POST /api/license/credits` accepts `amount` straight off the wire (bounded only by `validateCreditDeductionAmount`: finite, >0, ≤1,000,000, 2 dp) and deducts it — with the license key as the sole credential and no rate limit, so anyone holding a leaked key can zero a balance in one request. `session.metadata.credit_amount` is trusted in `handleCreditPurchase`; confirm nothing but `/api/checkout/credits` can write that metadata. Confirm `computeCreditPurchase()` is the only source of `creditCents`/`feeCents` and that `components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx:92` sends only `amount`, never a price.\n\n### I7: Every unauthenticated Next.js route handler whose work fans out to a paid third party or writes a durable object is rate-limited.\nWhy it matters: one cheap POST maps 1:1 to a Polar call, a Stripe customer/session creation, or a 6-provider model fan-out — free amplification and quota/cost burn against an open endpoint.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rLn \"RateLimiter\\|\\.limit(\" nextjs/app/api/**/route.ts` and compare against `nextjs/lib/rate-limit.ts` (only `downloadEmailRateLimiter` and `licenseValidateRateLimiter` exist). `/api/license/validate` and `/api/license/activate` are covered; `/api/license/credits` (GET **and** the deducting POST), `/api/checkout/credits` (creates real `stripe.customers` + Checkout Sessions), `/api/download` (outbound appcast fetch + dynamic RegExp), and `/api/internal/models` are not. For `/api/internal/models` and `/models`, verify the `s-maxage=3600` header plus `fetchAvailableModels`'s in-memory TTL actually bound the fan-out on a cache-busted or cold-lambda request.\n\n### I8: Any handler returning or mutating a specific user's data derives that user from the Better Auth session (or an admin check), never from a client-supplied id; middleware cookie-presence is never the only gate.\nWhy it matters: `middleware.ts` checks only that `better-auth.session_token` **exists** for `/{locale}/user/*` — a forged or expired cookie value passes it. If a page or procedure then trusts that, an unauthenticated caller reads customer data.\nSearch strategy: for each of `app/[locale]/user/(authenticated)/{dashboard,devices,customers}/page.tsx` and `layout.tsx`, grep for `auth.api.getSession`; any page that renders account data without one relies on the presence-only check. Cross-check the tRPC routers: `grep -n \"publicProcedure\\|protectedProcedure\\|adminProcedure\" nextjs/server/api/routers/**/*.ts` — every `admin/*` procedure must use `adminProcedure` (`ctx.isAdmin` from `user.role === \"admin\"`), and every `customer.*` procedure must key off `ctx.user.id`/`ctx.user.email`, never an `input`-supplied id. Also verify `src/db/schema/auth.ts` marks `role` as `input: false` so it cannot be set at sign-up.\n\n### I9: Every comparison of a caller-supplied credential against an env secret is timing-safe **and** fails closed when the env var is unset or empty.\nWhy it matters: `timingSafeEqualSecret(received, expected)` returns `false` when `expected` is falsy — good — but any `===` comparison, or a route that skips the check when the env var is missing, silently opens an admin/mint endpoint in a misconfigured environment.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"process.env\\..*SECRET\\|process.env\\..*TOKEN\\|process.env\\..*KEY\" nextjs/app/api nextjs/lib | grep -n \"===\\|!==\\|==\"`. Confirm all three `/api/internal/*` routes call `timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)` before any DB work (they do at line ~11 of each). In `app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts`, check the interaction between `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET` and `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN`: when signing is enabled, verify the bearer path cannot be used as an alternative bypass, and that a missing `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` doesn't make `timingSafeEqualSecret` the only barrier to a mint-shaped mutation. Also `app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts:33-40` correctly 500s on a missing `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET` — confirm no sibling handler does the opposite.\n\n### I10: Every `fetch()` to a third-party API carries an `AbortSignal.timeout` or an `AbortController`.\nWhy it matters: a hung upstream leaves an unresolved promise pinning the request scope — on a 1 GB Fly machine that bloats the Bun worker until OOM-kill, and on Vercel it burns the full function max-duration.\nSearch strategy: `grep -rn \"fetch(\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src nextjs/lib nextjs/app | grep -v \"signal:\"`. In the cloud service all 10 STT adapters route through `fetchWithTimeout()` in `providers/utils.ts` (default `DEFAULT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000`), but **all 7 LLM provider files — `anthropic.ts`, `cerebras.ts`, `groq-llm.ts`, `gemini-llm.ts`, `mistral-llm.ts`, `openai-llm.ts`, `xai-llm.ts` — use bare `fetch()` with no timeout** (`streamAnthropicChat`'s AbortController fires only on client disconnect, not on a stall). Note `gcs-storage.ts` maintains a *second, duplicated* `fetchWithTimeoutMs` implementation that can drift from `providers/utils.ts`. On the Next.js side check `lib/services/model-list.ts` `apiGet()` and the Polar SDK calls in `src/lib/license-validation.ts` (no timeout is configurable there, yet it sits behind an unauthenticated endpoint).\n\n### I11: In the WebSocket bridge, every acquired resource is released on every path, and closing one side always closes the other.\nWhy it matters: an orphaned upstream Deepgram socket keeps metering billable audio and holds a Fly file descriptor; a leaked `setInterval` survives the request.\nSearch strategy: read `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` end to end and check each early `return`. The `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES` breach at line 308 sends an error and calls `clientSocket.close(1009)` but **never calls `closeUpstream()` / `deepgramWs.close()`** — verify whether the resulting client close reliably fires `onClose`. Also: `pingInterval` is cleared only inside `endSession()`; the `apiKey`-missing early return at line 238 sets it never, but confirm no path sets the interval then exits without `endSession()`. Grep `setInterval(\\|clearInterval(\\|getReader()\\|releaseLock()\\|\\.cancel(` across `src/` — `providers/anthropic.ts`'s `streamAnthropicChat` `start()` catch block never calls `reader.cancel()` when the failure is not a client abort.\n\n### I12: The provider and model reported back to the client (`X-STT-Provider`, `X-STT-Model`, deduction `metadata`) always names the provider/model that actually ran and was priced.\nWhy it matters: `transcribe.ts:669` does `result.source as Provider` and looks the price up from that; if an adapter returns a `source` that isn't its own registry id, or reports a `model` it didn't run, the charge is computed against the wrong rate table and support has no audit trail.\nSearch strategy: `grep -n \"source:\" hyperwhisper-cloud/src/providers/*.ts` and check each returned literal against `TranscriptionSource` in `providers/types.ts` **and** against `SttProviderId` in `lib/stt-models.ts` — these are two parallel unions that must stay in sync (`TranscriptionSource` additionally carries `'no_speech'`). Then confirm `usedModel = result.model || attemptModel` (line 511) is what reaches both the `X-STT-Model` header and `stt_model` in the `deductCredits` metadata, and that `getProviderDef(current).defaultModel` on a fallback hop matches the rate actually used in `estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks`. Also check `cost-calculator.ts`'s `rates[model] ?? rates[defaultModel]` fallback — an unknown model id is silently re-priced at the default rate.\n\n### I13: Every value crossing a trust boundary is validated (or allow-listed/escaped) before it is used to index, interpolate, allocate, or render.\nWhy it matters: these are the crash-and-injection sinks — an unvalidated `req.json()` destructure 500s, an unescaped name lands script in a transactional email from a trusted sender, an unbounded `Content-Length` allocates memory before auth.\nSearch strategy: (a) `grep -rn \"await req.json()\\|await request.json()\\|c.req.json()\" nextjs/app/api hyperwhisper-cloud/src` and confirm each result is shape-checked before destructuring — `app/api/license/credits/route.ts:82` does `const body = await req.json(); const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;` with no null guard and an entirely unvalidated `metadata` that is then logged. WEBSOCKET STREAMING ROUTE\n// GET /ws/streaming-deepgram - Deepgram Live proxy\n\nimport type { Context, Next } from 'hono';\nimport { upgradeWebSocket } from 'hono/bun';\nimport { generateRequestId, getClientIP } from '../lib/request-id';\nimport { computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost, creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { validateAuth, type AuthContext } from '../middleware/auth';\nimport { deductCredits, validateCredits } from '../middleware/credits';\nimport { isIPBlocked } from '../lib/redis';\n\ninterface DeepgramLiveResponse {\n  type: string;\n  duration?: number;\n  is_final?: boolean;\n  speech_final?: boolean;\n  channel?: {\n    alternatives?: Array<{ transcript?: string }>;\n  };\n}\n\ninterface ReadyMessage {\n  type: 'ready';\n  sessionId: string;\n}\n\ninterface TranscriptMessage {\n  type: 'transcript';\n  text: string;\n  is_final: boolean;\n  speech_final: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface SessionCompleteMessage {\n  type: 'session_complete';\n  duration_seconds: number;\n  credits_used: number;\n}\n\ninterface ErrorMessage {\n  type: 'error';\n  message: string;\n}\n\ntype ServerMessage = ReadyMessage | TranscriptMessage | SessionCompleteMessage | ErrorMessage;\n\nconst STREAMING_SAMPLE_RATE = 16000;\nconst STREAMING_CHANNELS = 1;\nconst LINEAR16_BYTES_PER_SAMPLE = 2;\n\n// Minimum balance required to open a streaming session (~30s of Deepgram Nova-3 audio).\nconst STREAMING_MIN_BALANCE_SECONDS = 30;\n\nexport function minimumStreamingCredits(): number {\n  return creditsForCost(computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(STREAMING_MIN_BALANCE_SECONDS));\n}\n\n// Inbound audio limits — guard the Deepgram proxy against a misbehaving or\n// malicious client that pushes binary far faster (or larger) than the natural\n// 32 KB/s rate of 16 kHz mono linear16. Without these caps the client can grow\n// the outbound socket's buffer unbounded until the Fly machine OOMs.\n//\n// A single audio frame above 1 MB (~32 s of audio) is abnormal for streaming.\nconst MAX_AUDIO_MESSAGE_BYTES = 1 * 1024 * 1024;\n// Cumulative per-session cap (~52 min of 16 kHz mono linear16), well above any\n// real dictation session. Exceeding it closes the socket with 1009.\nconst MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024;\n// If the upstream socket has more than this still buffered, the client is\n// outrunning Deepgram — drop the chunk instead of queueing more memory.\nconst MAX_DEEPGRAM_BUFFERED_BYTES = 1 * 1024 * 1024;\n\ninterface WSContext {\n  readyState: number;\n  send(data: string | ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array): void;\n  close(code?: number, reason?: string): void;\n}\n\ndeclare module 'hono' {\n  interface ContextVariableMap {\n    wsAuth: AuthContext;\n    wsClientIP: string;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction sendToClient(socket: WSContext, message: ServerMessage): void {\n  if (socket.readyState === 1) {\n    socket.send(JSON.stringify(message));\n  }\n}\n\nexport function durationSecondsForLinear16AudioBytes(byteLength: number): number {\n  return byteLength / (STREAMING_SAMPLE_RATE * STREAMING_CHANNELS * LINEAR16_BYTES_PER_SAMPLE);\n}\n\nfunction buildDeepgramUrl(language?: string, vocabulary?: string): string {\n  const params = new URLSearchParams({\n    model: 'nova-3',\n    smart_format: 'true',\n    interim_results: 'true',\n    punctuate: 'true',\n    endpointing: '300',\n    encoding: 'linear16',\n    sample_rate: '16000',\n    channels: '1',\n    mip_opt_out: 'true',\n  });\n\n  const normalizedLanguage = language?.toLowerCase();\n  if (normalizedLanguage && normalizedLanguage !== 'auto') {\n    params.set('language', normalizedLanguage);\n    if (vocabulary) {\n      // Nova-3 `keyterm` takes ONE repeated query value per term — `keyterm=a&\n      // keyterm=b` — and does NOT support a `:boost` suffix (that's the legacy\n      // nova-2 `keywords` syntax). A single comma-joined `a:1.5,b:1.5` value\n      // boosts one literal phrase and does nothing. Split/clean to match the\n      // REST adapter's convertToKeyterms, then append each term individually.\n      const terms = vocabulary\n        .split(/[,\\n;]+/)\n        .map(t => t.trim().replace(/^[-*]\\s*/, ''))\n        .filter(t => t.length > 0 && t.length <= 50)\n        .slice(0, 100);\n      for (const term of terms) {\n        params.append('keyterm', term);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return `wss://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?${params.toString()}`;\n}\n\nexport async function wsStreamingPreflight(c: Context, next: Next) {\n  const upgradeHeader = c.req.header('Upgrade');\n  if (!upgradeHeader || upgradeHeader.toLowerCase() !== 'websocket') {\n    return c.text('Expected WebSocket upgrade', 426);\n  }\n\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    return c.text('Access denied', 403);\n  }\n\n  const url = new URL(c.req.url);\n  // `account_key` is the canonical param; `license_key` is the legacy alias that\n  // installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const licenseKey =\n    url.searchParams.get('account_key') ||\n    url.searchParams.get('license_key') ||\n    undefined;\n\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    return c.text('Missing account_key', 401);\n  }\n\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({ licenseKey });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    return c.text('Unauthorized', 401);\n  }\n\n  const creditCheck = await validateCredits(authResult.value, minimumStreamingCredits(), clientIP);\n  if (!creditCheck.ok) {\n    return creditCheck.response;\n  }\n\n  c.set('wsAuth', authResult.value);\n  c.set('wsClientIP', clientIP);\n\n  return next();\n}\n\nexport const wsStreamingRoute = upgradeWebSocket((c) => {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const auth = c.get('wsAuth');\n  const clientIP = c.get('wsClientIP');\n  const url = new URL(c.req.url);\n  const language = url.searchParams.get('language') || undefined;\n  const vocabulary = url.searchParams.get('vocabulary') || undefined;\n  const apiKey = process.env.DEEPGRAM_API_KEY || '';\n\n  let totalDurationSeconds = 0;\n  let bytesReceived = 0;\n  let deepgramWs: WebSocket | null = null;\n  let sessionEnded = false;\n  let clientSocket: WSContext | null = null;\n  let pingInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\n\n  const dgUrl = buildDeepgramUrl(language, vocabulary);\n\n  async function endSession(): Promise<void> {\n    if (sessionEnded) return;\n    sessionEnded = true;\n\n    if (pingInterval) {\n      clearInterval(pingInterval);\n      pingInterval = null;\n    }\n\n    const costUsd = computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(totalDurationSeconds);\n    const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n    if (clientSocket) {\n      sendToClient(clientSocket, {\n        type: 'session_complete',\n        duration_seconds: totalDurationSeconds,\n        credits_used: creditsUsed,\n      });\n    }\n\n    if (creditsUsed > 0) {\n      deductCredits(\n        auth,\n        costUsd,\n        {\n          audio_duration_seconds: totalDurationSeconds,\n          transcription_cost_usd: costUsd,\n          language: language || 'auto',\n          endpoint: '/ws/streaming-deepgram',\n          stt_provider: 'deepgram-nova3-live',\n        },\n        clientIP\n      ).catch(console.error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  function closeUpstream(): void {\n    // readyState 0 = CONNECTING, 1 = OPEN — close both so a client that\n    // disconnects mid-handshake doesn't leave the upstream socket open until\n    // Deepgram's idle timeout. close() during CONNECTING aborts the handshake\n    // once it completes; CLOSING/CLOSED need no action.\n    if (deepgramWs && deepgramWs.readyState <= WebSocket.OPEN) {\n      deepgramWs.close(1000, 'Client disconnected');\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    onOpen: (_evt, ws) => {\n      clientSocket = ws;\n\n      if (!apiKey) {\n        sendToClient(ws, { type: 'error', message: 'Deepgram API key not configured' });\n        ws.close(1011, 'Configuration error');\n        return;\n      }\n\n      deepgramWs = new WebSocket(dgUrl, ['token', apiKey]);\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('open', () => {\n        // If the client already disconnected while we were still handshaking,\n        // tear down the upstream socket instead of leaving it orphaned.\n        if (ws.readyState !== 1) {\n          closeUpstream();\n          return;\n        }\n        sendToClient(ws, { type: 'ready', sessionId: requestId });\n      });\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('message', (event) => {\n        try {\n          const data = JSON.parse(event.data as string) as DeepgramLiveResponse;\n          if (data.type === 'Results') {\n            const transcript = data.channel?.alternatives?.[0]?.transcript || '';\n            if (transcript || data.is_final) {\n              sendToClient(ws, {\n                type: 'transcript',\n                text: transcript,\n                is_final: data.is_final ?? false,\n                speech_final: data.speech_final ?? false,\n              });\n            }\n          }\n        } catch (error) {\n          console.warn('Failed to parse Deepgram message', error);\n        }\n      });\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('error', () => {\n        sendToClient(ws, { type: 'error', message: 'Transcription service error' });\n      });\n\n      deepgramWs.addEventListener('close', async () => {\n        await endSession();\n        if (ws.readyState === 1) {\n          ws.close(1000, 'Session ended');\n        }\n      });\n\n      // Send ping every 30s to prevent Fly.io's 60s idle timeout from killing the connection\n      pingInterval = setInterval(() => {\n        if (clientSocket && clientSocket.readyState === 1) {\n          clientSocket.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'ping' }));\n        }\n      }, 30000);\n    },\n    onMessage: (event) => {\n      if (!deepgramWs || deepgramWs.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const data = event.data;\n      if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {\n        // Count every inbound frame toward the session total first — even ones\n        // we reject below — so a flood of oversized frames still trips the\n        // cumulative cap and closes the socket instead of looping forever.\n        bytesReceived += data.byteLength;\n\n        // Bound total inbound volume so a flood can't OOM the worker. Checked\n        // before the per-frame size guard so oversized frames also count here\n        // and a sustained flood reliably closes the connection.\n        if (bytesReceived > MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Audio stream too large' });\n            clientSocket.close(1009, 'Message too big');\n          }\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // Reject an abnormally large single frame outright — never forward it.\n        if (data.byteLength > MAX_AUDIO_MESSAGE_BYTES) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Audio chunk too large' });\n          }\n          return;\n        }\n\n        // Backpressure: if the upstream socket is already congested, drop this\n        // chunk instead of queueing more memory into the outbound buffer.\n        if (deepgramWs.bufferedAmount > MAX_DEEPGRAM_BUFFERED_BYTES) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Transcription service busy, audio dropped' });\n          }\n          return;\n        }\n\n        deepgramWs.send(data);\n        totalDurationSeconds += durationSecondsForLinear16AudioBytes(data.byteLength);\n\n        // End the session once the running cost reaches the balance seen at auth,\n        // so a low-balance user can't stream indefinitely on end-of-session billing.\n        const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(totalDurationSeconds));\n        if (creditsUsed >= auth.credits) {\n          if (clientSocket) {\n            sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'Credit balance exhausted' });\n          }\n          deepgramWs.close(1000, 'Credits exhausted');\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (typeof data === 'string') {\n        try {\n          const msg = JSON.parse(data) as { type?: string };\n          if (msg.type === 'stop') {\n            deepgramWs.close(1000, 'Client requested stop');\n            return;\n          }\n          if (msg.type === 'pong') {\n            // Client pong response — ignore\n            return;\n          }\n        } catch {\n          // ignore non-JSON text messages\n        }\n      }\n    },\n    onClose: async () => {\n      await endSession();\n      closeUpstream();\n    },\n    onError: async () => {\n      if (clientSocket) {\n        sendToClient(clientSocket, { type: 'error', message: 'WebSocket error' });\n      }\n      await endSession();\n      closeUpstream();\n    },\n  };\n});\n","numLines":377,"startLine":1,"totalLines":377}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"406332bb-ef23-4a15-97d3-3d807d2eaf70","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n2\t// Handles preflight credit checks and post-usage deduction\n3\t\n4\timport type { AuthContext } from './auth';\n5\timport { BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE, CREDITS_PER_MINUTE, DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\n6\timport { roundToTenth, roundUpToTenth } from '../lib/utils';\n7\timport { creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\n8\timport { insufficientCreditsResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n9\timport { cacheLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n10\t\n11\t// Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation.\n12\tconst MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS = 10;\n13\t\n14\texport type CreditsResult =\n15\t  | { ok: true }\n16\t  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n17\t\n18\texport interface CreditEstimateOptions {\n19\t  costEstimators?: Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n20\t}\n21\t\n22\texport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: number): number {\n23\t  const estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;\n24\t  return Math.max(MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS, estimatedMinutes * 60);\n25\t}\n26\t\n27\texport function estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n28\t  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n29\t\n30\t  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n31\t    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n32\t      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n33\t    );\n34\t    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n35\t  }\n36\t\n37\t  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n38\t  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n39\t}\n40\t\n41\texport async function validateCredits(\n42\t  auth: AuthContext,\n43\t  estimatedCredits: number,\n44\t  _clientIP: string\n45\t): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n46\t  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n47\t  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n48\t    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n49\t  }\n50\t  return { ok: true };\n51\t}\n52\t\n53\tasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n54\t  licenseKey: string,\n55\t  creditsUsed: number,\n56\t  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n57\t): Promise<void> {\n58\t  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n59\t\n60\t  try {\n61\t    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n62\t      method: 'POST',\n63\t      headers: {\n64\t        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n65\t      },\n66\t      body: JSON.stringify({\n67\t CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n// Handles preflight credit checks and post-usage deduction\n\nimport type { AuthContext } from './auth';\nimport { BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE, CREDITS_PER_MINUTE, DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { roundToTenth, roundUpToTenth } from '../lib/utils';\nimport { creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { insufficientCreditsResponse } from '../lib/responses';\nimport { cacheLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n\n// Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation.\nconst MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS = 10;\n\nexport type CreditsResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\nexport interface CreditEstimateOptions {\n  costEstimators?: Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n}\n\nexport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: number): number {\n  const estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;\n  return Math.max(MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS, estimatedMinutes * 60);\n}\n\nexport function estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n\n  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n    );\n    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n  }\n\n  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n}\n\nexport async function validateCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  estimatedCredits: number,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n  }\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditsUsed: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        amount: creditsUsed,\n        metadata,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      console.warn('POST /api/license/credits failed', {\n        status: response.status,\n        error: (errorData as Record<string, unknown>).error || 'Unknown error',\n        creditsUsed,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const data = await response.json() as { credits_remaining?: number; credits_deducted?: number };\n    if (typeof data.credits_remaining === 'number') {\n      await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n        isValid: true,\n        credits: data.credits_remaining,\n        cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('POST /api/license/credits network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"244df5fc-147c-4e78-8999-be1302e934b5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"a8e51784-fc49-487e-be1b-563c62dd2fc2","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a5d34dd62f848ca0a","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01E124HPCjjkys7Zd2ft61ur","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// AUTHENTICATION HELPERS\n2\t// Validates license keys and device trial identifiers\n3\t\n4\timport { DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\n5\timport { cacheLicense, getCachedLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n6\timport { invalidLicenseResponse, licenseRequiredResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n7\t\n8\texport interface AuthContext {\n9\t  identifier: string; // license key\n10\t  credits: number;\n11\t  licenseKey: string;\n12\t}\n13\t\n14\texport interface AuthInput {\n15\t  licenseKey?: string;\n16\t}\n17\t\n18\texport type AuthResult =\n19\t  | { ok: true; value: AuthContext }\n20\t  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n21\t\n22\t// Mask license key for logging (show first 4 and last 4 chars)\n23\tfunction maskLicenseKey(key: string): string {\n24\t  if (key.length <= 8) return '****';\n25\t  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}...${key.slice(-4)}`;\n26\t}\n27\t\n28\tasync function validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; credits: number }> {\n29\t  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n30\t  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n31\t  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n32\t\n33\t  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n34\t\n35\t  try {\n36\t    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n37\t      method: 'POST',\n38\t      headers: {\n39\t        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n40\t      },\n41\t      body: JSON.stringify({\n42\t        license_key: licenseKey,\n43\t        include_credits: true,\n44\t      }),\n45\t      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n46\t    });\n47\t\n48\t    const responseText = await response.text();\n49\t    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n50\t\n51\t    try {\n52\t      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n53\t    } catch {\n54\t      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n55\t    }\n56\t\n57\t    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n58\t    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n59\t\n60\t    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n61\t\n62\t    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n63\t    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n64\t    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n65\t    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n66\t    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n67\t    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n68\t    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n69\t    // the licensing API on every call.\n70\t    if (response.status >= 500) {\n71\t      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n72\t      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n73\t    }\n74\t\n75\t    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n76\t      isValid,\n77\t      credits,\n78\t      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n79\t    });\n80\t\n81\t    return { isValid, credits };\n82\t  } catch (error) {\n83\t    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n84\t    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n85\t    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. Fail this request\n86\t    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n87\t    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n88\t      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n89\t    } else {\n90\t      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n91\t    }\n92\t    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n93\t  }\n94\t}\n95\t\n96\texport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n97\t  const { licenseKey } = input;\n98\t\n99\t  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n100\t  // credit balance) is required for every request. There is no anonymous/trial\n101\t  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n102\t  if (!licenseKey) {\n103\t    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n104\t    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n105\t  }\n106\t\n107\t  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n108\t\n109\t  if (!forceRefresh) {\n110\t    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n111\t    if (cached) {\n112\t      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, AUTHENTICATION HELPERS\n// Validates license keys and device trial identifiers\n\nimport { DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { cacheLicense, getCachedLicense } from '../lib/redis';\nimport { invalidLicenseResponse, licenseRequiredResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n\nexport interface AuthContext {\n  identifier: string; // license key\n  credits: number;\n  licenseKey: string;\n}\n\nexport interface AuthInput {\n  licenseKey?: string;\n}\n\nexport type AuthResult =\n  | { ok: true; value: AuthContext }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\n// Mask license key for logging (show first 4 and last 4 chars)\nfunction maskLicenseKey(key: string): string {\n  if (key.length <= 8) return '****';\n  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}...${key.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nasync function validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; credits: number }> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        include_credits: true,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const responseText = await response.text();\n    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n\n    try {\n      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n    } catch {\n      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n    }\n\n    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n\n    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n\n    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n    // the licensing API on every call.\n    if (response.status >= 500) {\n      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n    }\n\n    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n      isValid,\n      credits,\n      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    return { isValid, credits };\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. Fail this request\n    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n    } else {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n    }\n    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n  const { licenseKey } = input;\n\n  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n  // credit balance) is required for every request. There is no anonymous/trial\n  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n  }\n\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  if (!forceRefresh) {\n    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, cachedAt=${cached.cachedAt}`);\n      if (!cached.isValid) {\n        return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n      }\n      return {\n        ok: true,\n        value: {\n          identifier: licenseKey,\n          credits: cached.credits,\n          licenseKey,\n        },\n      };\n    }\n    console.log(`[Auth] Cache MISS for ${maskedKey}, calling API...`);\n  } else {\n    console.log(`[Auth] Force refresh for ${maskedKey}, bypassing cache...`);\n  }\n\n  const validation = await validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey);\n  if (!validation.isValid) {\n    console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is INVALID`);\n    return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n  }\n\n  console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is VALID with ${validation.credits} credits`);\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    value: {\n      identifier: licenseKey,\n      credits: validation.credits,\n      licenseKey,\n    },\n  };\n}\n","numLines":146,"startLine":1,"totalLines":146}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a8e51784-fc49-487e-be1b-563c62dd2fc2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Nothing is ever forwarded to Deepgram (`deepgramWs.send` at line 333 is never reached), so `totalDurationSeconds` stays exactly `0`.\n\n**Decisive lines.** The accumulator is a plain float, not a `creditsForCost` result — it is only ever incremented inside the forward branch:\n\n```ts\n// line 179\nlet totalDurationSeconds = 0;\n...\n// line 333-334  (only mutation site, after the send)\ndeepgramWs.send(data);\ntotalDurationSeconds += durationSecondsForLinear16AudioBytes(data.byteLength);\n```\n\nso it is genuinely `0` on this path. But the conversion floors:\n\n```ts\n// line 197-198\nconst costUsd = computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(totalDurationSeconds); // roundUsd(0) === 0\nconst creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);                            // === 0.1\n...\n// line 208\nif (creditsUsed > 0) {\n```\n\n`cost-calculator.ts:496-499`:\n```ts\nexport function creditsForCost(costUsd: number): number {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(costUsd) || costUsd <= 0) {\n    return 0.1;\n  }\n```\n\nThe guard is vacuous. `deductCredits(auth, 0, …)` → `credits.ts:138-144` recomputes `creditsForCost(0) === 0.1`, so the `creditsUsed <= 0` early return does not fire either, and `recordLicenseUsage(licenseKey, 0.1, {audio_duration_seconds: 0, …})` POSTs to `/api/license/credits`. `validateCreditDeductionAmount` (`nextjs/app/api/license/credits/validation.ts:9-22`) accepts `0.1` (finite, >0, ≤1e6, 2dp), so a real 0.1-credit ledger spend lands. The client simultaneously receives `session_complete {duration_seconds: 0, credits_used: 0.1}` (line 201-205, delivered here because the client socket is still `readyState === 1`), so the defect is directly user-visible.\n\nSame deduction fires on the misconfiguration path the candidate names (`onOpen` line 238-242, `!apiKey` → `ws.close(1011)` → hono's bun adapter `websocket.close()` → `onClose` → `endSession()`) and on a failed Deepgram handshake.\n\n**Intent check — not intentional.** `git blame -L 208,208` gives only the single vendoring commit `3b810e6` (no incremental history). No comment defends a minimum session charge. The decisive counter-evidence is the author's own working idiom for exactly this guard one file over, `transcribe.ts:676-678`, where the zero is injected explicitly precisely because `creditsForCost` cannot produce one:\n```ts\nconst creditsUsed = noSpeech ? 0 : creditsForCost(result.costUsd);\nif (!noSpeech) { deductCredits(...) }\n```\n`ws-streaming-deepgram.ts` has no such injection. `ws-streaming-deepgram.test.ts` is 18 lines covering only `durationSecondsForLinear16AudioBytes`; `cost-calculator.test.ts` pins no `creditsForCost` floor behaviour. Nothing pins this.\n\n*Correction to the candidate's parenthetical:* `estimateCreditsForCost` (line 505, the sibling that returns `0`) is **dead code** — `grep` shows zero call sites outside `cost-calculator.ts`. The \"two siblings disagree\" remark is true but has no operational consequence.\n\n---\n\n## `[hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts:188]` **PLAUSIBLE**\n\nThe code mechanism is fully verified; the trigger is an external platform event I cannot construct.\n\n**Verified in code:**\n- `drainPendingDeductions` (`credits.ts:106-116`) drains only `inFlightDeductions`, and the sole insertion point is `deductCredits` (`credits.ts:125`). A live session has not called it — `deductCredits` is reached only from `endSession()` line 209. So for a machine whose only work is one live WS session, `inFlightDeductions.size === 0` and the drain returns `0` **immediately**.\n- `gracefulShutdown` (`index.ts:85-100`) holds no registry of open sockets, never calls `endSession`, and ends in `process.exit(0)` — which terminates without running Bun's WS `close` handlers, and would not await the `recordLicenseUsage` fetch even if it did.\n- The client's own session-end cannot have fired first: SIGTERM sends the client no signal; it learns of the teardown only when the TCP connection dies at `process.exit`, by which point the handler is gone.\n- Fly does send SIGTERM with a grace period — the author documents it himself at `index.ts:78-80`: *\"Fly sends SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down with a grace period before SIGKILL (kill_timeout, default 5s)\"*. (No `fly.toml` for this service is in the repo; deploy config was moved to the app repo per commit `3b810e6`, so the actual `kill_timeout` is unverifiable here — but it is irrelevant, since the drain path exits without waiting regardless.)\n\n**Exact unproven link:** that a Fly deploy / scale-down / machine recycle actually overlaps a session that has accumulated non-trivial `totalDurationSeconds`. That is a deployment-frequency-vs-session-duration question, not a code question. Confirming it needs either production evidence (a `machine.shutdown` log line with concurrent open `/ws/streaming-deepgram` sockets, or a Deepgram invoice line with no matching `stt_provider: 'deepgram-nova3-live'` deduction) or the deploy cadence + concurrent-session count.\n\n**Magnitude if it fires:** the whole session, bounded by `min(auth.credits, ~300.4 credits)` — the 100 MiB cap is 3276.8 s ≈ 54.6 min ≈ $0.300 ≈ 300 credits.\n\n**Intent check — not deliberate.** `git blame` is again the single vendoring commit. The drain's own comment (`credits.ts:99-103`, `index.ts:76-81`) scopes it explicitly to *\"Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency) … between the response flush and the redis/license write\"* — HTTP-request-shaped language throughout; WebSockets are never mentioned in either comment. No test covers shutdown. This is an unhandled case, not a documented tradeoff.\n\n---\n\n## `[hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/ws-streaming-deepgram.ts:159]` **REFUTED**\n\nThe guard the candidate itself names, at line 336-344, is the load-bearing bound and it works.\n\n**Quantified gap.** Reservation: `creditsForCost(computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(30))` = `creditsForCost(0.00275)` = **2.8 credits**. Worst-case session at `MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES`: 104,857,600 B ÷ 32,000 B/s = 3276.8 s = 54.6 min × $0.0055/min = $0.3004 = **300.4 credits**. So the raw ratio is ~107x — but that ceiling is unreachable unless the user's balance already exceeds it.\n\n**The barrier.** Line 336-344, evaluated after *every* forwarded frame:\n```ts\n// End the session once the running cost reaches the balance seen at auth,\n// so a low-balance user can't stream indefinitely on end-of-session billing.\nconst creditsUsed = creditsForCost(computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(totalDurationSeconds));\nif (creditsUsed >= auth.credits) {\n  ...\n  deepgramWs.close(1000, 'Credits exhausted');\n}\n```\nOnce `close()` is called, `deepgramWs.readyState` leaves `OPEN`, so the very next frame returns at line 294-296 and nothing further is forwarded. Total deduction is therefore bounded by `auth.credits` plus at most one frame's worth of overshoot — capped by `MAX_AUDIO_MESSAGE_BYTES` = 1 MiB = 32.768 s = **~3.0 credits ≈ $0.003 per session**. That, not 300 credits, is the real unrecoverable exposure, and it is negligible.\n\n**The candidate's own failure scenario is not a failure.** \"a cached balance of a few hundred credits, streams 52 minutes, deducts ~286 credits\" — the user *has* those credits and is charged exactly for the Deepgram minutes consumed. Nothing floors at 0, the business absorbs nothing, and the user is not overcharged. I1's stated rationale (\"the only thing standing between a low-balance user and unbounded provider spend\") is satisfied by line 339, not by the preflight.\n\n**Intentional — the preflight is a minimum-balance gate, not a reservation, and says so.** `git blame -L 159,159` → single vendoring commit; the surrounding declaration is explicit:\n```ts\n// Minimum balance required to open a streaming session (~30s of Deepgram Nova-3 audio).\nconst STREAMING_MIN_BALANCE_SECONDS = 30;\nexport function minimumStreamingCredits(): number { ... }\n```\nBoth the constant name (`_MIN_BALANCE_`), the function name (`minimumStreamingCredits`) and the comment describe an entry floor. The comment at line 336-337 states the design directly: the in-session cap is what prevents indefinite streaming on end-of-session billing. Reserving a full session's worth up front is impossible for an open-ended stream, which is why the design splits gate-then-meter. Deliberate and load-bearing.\n\n**The stale-cache half of the claim is also weaker than stated.** `recordLicenseUsage` re-caches `credits_remaining` on every successful deduction (`credits.ts:84-91`), so the 1h `license:<key>` entry is refreshed by every billed request, and the cache is a single shared Upstash instance — the candidate's \"on another Fly region\" framing is wrong, all regions read the same key. The genuine residual is *concurrent* sessions: N sockets opened before any of them deducts each read the same `auth.credits = B` and each can independently stream up to B, of which only B is collectible. That is a real amplification, but it is a concurrency defect at line 339, not the reservation-size claim filed here, and it is not what this candidate asserts."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2258,"cache_read_input_tokens":85611,"output_tokens":4240,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2258},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":4240,"cache_read_input_tokens":85611,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2258,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2258,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWJAieHxcy4VHFtgb7DA","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"5b9de963-363a-4764-b21c-5ada6071076d","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:49:44.385Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
